Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a search algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 47. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 764/3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to search structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 47/764 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the search object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 272. The moduli space has dimension at most 399 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the structural decomposition decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_272(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 272. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^399(S, F) is trivial. QED.
